BUILDING FIT

BORROW

  • Pinned source findings expose the control loop
  • Context, tools, and policy are separated into reviewable dimensions
  • Each chapter keeps a file and line-range trail

WATCH

  • Source claims are pinned to the checked-out commit
  • Deployment-level behavior still needs a fresh run
  • Legacy findings should be re-audited when the repository moves

READING MODE

  • Pin the commit before tracing the call chain
  • Separate facts, inferences, and risks
  • Every finding returns to code
00 · METHOD

Method: not a README summary

README POLICY

README locates entrypoints and naming; implementation claims return to runtime code, contracts, or tests.

FACT POLICY

L1 runtime code leads; L2 contracts and L3/L4 evidence cross-check; L5 inferences stay explicit.

02 · COVERAGE MAP

Coverage and evidence levels

Control loopverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已逐段审计 Agent.reply/reply_internal 与 SessionManager。

Model & contextverifiedL1 / L2

已审计 Provider trait、流合并和 Agent 消费逻辑。

Model & contextverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计阈值判定、工具结果裁剪、结构化摘要和溢出恢复。

Tool registration & executionverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计分类、检查、审批、并发汇流、结果配对。

Execution & sandboxverifiedL1 / L2

已核对 shell/edit 的实际系统调用与路径解析；策略门不等于 OS 隔离。

Security, approval & permissionsverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计安全、外连、对抗、权限、重复五级 inspector 顺序。

Instructions & extension surfaceverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计系统提示构建、项目指令、子目录 hints 与 Unicode 标签清洗。

Connectors, plugins & MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计工具发现缓存、命名空间、HTTP/OAuth、调用分发与元数据净化。

Instructions & extension surfaceverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

已审计技能目录优先级、文件逃逸防护、插件 hooks 与 MCP 注入。

Collaboration & sub-agentsverifiedL1 / L2

已审计子 Agent 的独立 Agent/Session 构造、工具选择和禁止递归委派。

Observability & replayverifiedL1 / L2

已审计 SQLite/WAL、usage ledger、tracing span 与可选遥测。

Recovery, tests & trade-offspartialL2 / L3

关键路径测试已定位；全仓测试与 benchmark 数量统计待补。

 1089      /// Local code review.
 1090      ///
 1091      /// Discovers `**/.agents/checks/*.md` subagent reviewers and
 1092      /// `**/.agents/REVIEW.md` scoped prompt overrides, builds a review
 1093      /// request from the working tree (or an explicit diff range), and
 1094      /// runs the review through goose.
 1095      #[command(about = "Review the current diff using goose")]
 1096      Review {
 1097          /// Diff range to review (e.g. "main...HEAD"). Defaults to the working
 1098          /// tree vs HEAD.
 1099          #[arg(value_name = "RANGE")]
 1100          range: Option<String>,
 1101
 1102          /// Path to a Markdown file with a custom base review prompt. Replaces
 1103          /// the embedded default prompt.
 1104          #[arg(long = "prompt", value_name = "FILE")]
 1105          prompt: Option<PathBuf>,
 1106
 1107          /// Default model used for the main review agent and for any check
 1108          /// that does not declare its own `model:` in frontmatter.
 1109          #[arg(long = "model", value_name = "MODEL")]
 1110          model: Option<String>,
 1111
 1112          /// Provider for the main review agent.
 1113          #[arg(long = "provider", value_name = "PROVIDER")]
 1114          provider: Option<String>,
 1115
 1116          /// Force every discovered check to use this model, regardless of
 1117          /// the check's own `model:` field.
 1118          #[arg(long = "override-model", value_name = "MODEL")]
 1119          override_model: Option<String>,
 1120
 1121          /// Default `turn-limit` for orchestrated main-pass subprocesses and
 1122          /// for checks that do not declare their own. Does not cap the legacy
 1123          /// `--no-orchestrate` in-process main agent.
 1124          #[arg(long = "turn-limit", value_name = "N")]
 1125          turn_limit: Option<usize>,
 1126
 1127          /// Print the assembled review prompt and discovered checks instead of
 1128          /// running the review.
 1129          #[arg(long = "dry-run")]
 1130          dry_run: bool,
 1131
 1132          /// Suppress non-result output from the underlying agent.
 1133          #[arg(long, short = 'q')]
 1134          quiet: bool,
 1135
 1136          /// Disable the Rust-driven parallel orchestrator and fall back to
 1137          /// the single-prompt path that asks the main agent to delegate
 1138          /// each check via `delegate(... async: true ...)`. The default
 1139          /// orchestrator dispatches one `goose run` subprocess per check
 1140          /// (capped at 4 concurrent), bounding wall-clock to the slowest

  136      )]
  137      pub container: Option<String>,
  138  }
  139
  140  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  141  pub struct StreamableHttpOptions {
  142      pub url: String,
  143      pub timeout: u64,
  144  }
  145
  146  fn parse_streamable_http_extension(input: &str) -> Result<StreamableHttpOptions, String> {
  147      let mut input_iter = input.split_whitespace();
  148      let (mut url, mut timeout) = (String::new(), goose::config::DEFAULT_EXTENSION_TIMEOUT);
  149
  150      if let Some(url_str) = input_iter.next() {
  151          url.push_str(url_str);
  152      }
  153
  154      for kv_pair in input_iter {
  155          if !kv_pair.contains('=') {
  156              continue;
  157          }
  158
  159          let (key, value) = kv_pair.split_once('=').unwrap();
  160
  161          // We Can have more keys here for setting other properties
  162          if key == "timeout" {
  163              if let Ok(seconds) = value.parse::<u64>() {
  164                  timeout = seconds;
  165              }
  166          }
  167      }
  168
  169      Ok(StreamableHttpOptions { url, timeout })
  170  }
  171
  172  /// Extension configuration options shared between Session and Run commands
  173  #[derive(Args, Debug, Clone, Default)]
  174  pub struct ExtensionOptions {
  175      #[arg(
  176          long = "with-extension",
  177          value_name = "COMMAND",
  178          help = "Add stdio extensions (can be specified multiple times)",
  179          long_help = "Add stdio extensions from full commands with environment variables. Can be specified multiple times. Format: 'ENV1=val1 ENV2=val2 command args...'",
  180          action = clap::ArgAction::Append
  181      )]
  182      pub extensions: Vec<String>,
  183
  184      #[arg(
  185          long = "with-streamable-http-extension",
  186          value_name = "URL",
  187          help = "Add streamable HTTP extensions (can be specified multiple times)",
  188          long_help = "Add streamable HTTP extensions from a URL. Can be specified multiple times. Format: 'url...' or 'url... timeout=100' to set up timeout other than default",
  189          action = clap::ArgAction::Append,
  190          value_parser = parse_streamable_http_extension
  191      )]
  192      pub streamable_http_extensions: Vec<StreamableHttpOptions>,
Open this command's dynamic execution map →

 1317  impl CompletionShell {
 1318      fn generate(self, cmd: &mut clap::Command, bin_name: &str, writer: &mut dyn std::io::Write) {
 1319          match self {
 1320              CompletionShell::Bash => generate(ClapShell::Bash, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1321              CompletionShell::Elvish => generate(ClapShell::Elvish, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1322              CompletionShell::Fish => generate(ClapShell::Fish, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1323              CompletionShell::Powershell => generate(ClapShell::PowerShell, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1324              CompletionShell::Nu => generate(ClapNushell, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1325              CompletionShell::Zsh => generate(ClapShell::Zsh, cmd, bin_name, writer),
 1326          }

node

CLI subcommand · Rust command constructor · crates/goose-cli/src/commands/tui.rs:48
Source ↗

A CLI subcommand; argv is parsed into structured arguments before its handler runs.

node→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
   43  }
   44
   45  fn build_command(source: &TuiSource, args: &[String]) -> Result<Command> {
   46      match source {
   47          TuiSource::LocalScript(script) => {
   48              let mut cmd = Command::new("node");
   49              cmd.arg(script).args(args);
   50              Ok(cmd)
   51          }
   52          TuiSource::Npx(spec) => {
   53              let mut cmd = Command::new("npx");
   54              cmd.arg("--yes")
   55                  .arg("--package")
   56                  .arg(spec)
   57                  .arg("--")
   58                  .arg(NPM_BIN_NAME)
   59                  .args(args);
   60              Ok(cmd)
   61          }
   62      }
   63  }
   64
   65  pub fn handle_tui(args: Vec<String>) -> Result<()> {
   66      let source = resolve_source();
   67
   68      let goose_binary = std::env::current_exe()
   69          .context("could not determine current goose executable to expose as GOOSE_BINARY")?;
   70
   71      let mut cmd = build_command(&source, &args)?;
   72      cmd.env("GOOSE_BINARY", &goose_binary);
   73
   74      let descriptor = match &source {
   75          TuiSource::LocalScript(p) => format!("node {}", p.display()),
   76          TuiSource::Npx(spec) => format!("npx --package {} -- {}", spec, NPM_BIN_NAME),
   77      };
   78
   79      #[cfg(unix)]
   80      {
   81          use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
   82          let err = cmd.exec();
   83          Err(anyhow!("failed to exec TUI ({descriptor}): {err}"))
   84      }
   85
   86      #[cfg(not(unix))]
   87      {
   88          let status = cmd
   89              .status()
   90              .with_context(|| format!("failed to run `{descriptor}`"))?;
   91          if !status.success() {
   92              std::process::exit(status.code().unwrap_or(1));
   93          }
   94          Ok(())
   95      }
   96  }
   97
   98  #[cfg(test)]
   99  mod tests {
  100      use super::*;
  101      use std::fs;
  102
  103      #[test]
  104      fn find_local_script_ignores_unrelated_directories() {
  105          let temp_dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("create temp dir");
  106          let executable = temp_dir.path().join("install/bin/goose");
  107          let planted_script = temp_dir.path().join("checkout").join(TUI_REL_PATH);

   59
   60  pub struct ParsedSlashCommand<'a> {
   61      pub command: &'a str,
   62      pub params_str: &'a str,
   63  }
   64
   65  pub fn parse_slash_command(message_text: &str) -> Option<ParsedSlashCommand<'_>> {
   66      let mut trimmed = message_text.trim();
   67
   68      if COMPACT_TRIGGERS.contains(&trimmed) {
   69          trimmed = COMPACT_TRIGGERS[0];
   70      }
   71
   72      if !trimmed.starts_with('/') {
   73          return None;
   74      }
   75
   76      let command_str = trimmed.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or(trimmed);
   77      let (command, params_str) = command_str
   78          .split_once(' ')
   79          .map(|(cmd, p)| (cmd, p.trim()))
   80          .unwrap_or((command_str, ""));
   81
   82      Some(ParsedSlashCommand {
   83          command,
   84          params_str,
   85      })
   86  }
   87
   88  pub fn list_commands() -> &'static [CommandDef] {
   89      COMMANDS
   90  }
   91

   93      let Some(parsed) = parse_slash_command(message_text) else {

   59
   60  pub struct ParsedSlashCommand<'a> {
   61      pub command: &'a str,
   62      pub params_str: &'a str,
   63  }
   64
   65  pub fn parse_slash_command(message_text: &str) -> Option<ParsedSlashCommand<'_>> {
   66      let mut trimmed = message_text.trim();
   67
   68      if COMPACT_TRIGGERS.contains(&trimmed) {
   69          trimmed = COMPACT_TRIGGERS[0];
   70      }
   71
   72      if !trimmed.starts_with('/') {
   73          return None;
   74      }
   75
   76      let command_str = trimmed.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or(trimmed);
   77      let (command, params_str) = command_str
   78          .split_once(' ')
   79          .map(|(cmd, p)| (cmd, p.trim()))
   80          .unwrap_or((command_str, ""));
   81
   82      Some(ParsedSlashCommand {
   83          command,
   84          params_str,
   85      })

02
DIMENSION · LOOP

Control loop

2 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factloop

01 · 单一流式 Agent 循环驱动推理、工具和持久化

SOURCE FACT

reply_internal 在一个有界 loop 中调用 provider stream，消费增量消息，执行工具，把结果加入会话，再决定继续、重试、压缩或结束。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

它不是“模型回答一次就结束”，而是模型说一步、系统做一步、把结果再交回模型，直到满足结束条件。

IMPLICATION

Harness 的真正核心是状态机而不是提示词；重试、转向、停止钩子和上下文恢复都进入同一控制环。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1930–1985
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 1986–2043
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

CAVEAT / RISK
  • 默认最大 turn 是 1000，不代表每次任务都会接近该值；会话配置和环境参数可覆盖。

02
L1factloop

02 · 结束条件有防失控上限

SOURCE FACT

空模型响应最多重试 3 次；阻塞停止的插件 hook 连续超过可配置上限后会被强制覆盖，避免无限循环。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

即使模型什么都不返回，或插件一直说“还不能停”，Goose 也不会永远卡住。

IMPLICATION

所有“让 Agent 继续”的机制都需要配额和最终逃生门。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 67–72
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 73–79
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   86  fn categorize_tool(tool_name: &str) -> ToolCategory {
   87      let local = tool_name.rsplit("__").next().unwrap_or(tool_name);
   88      match local {
   89          "shell" | "bash" | "exec" | "run" => ToolCategory::Shell,
   90          "read" | "view" | "cat" | "read_file" => ToolCategory::Read,
   91          "write" | "edit" | "patch" | "write_file" | "edit_file" => ToolCategory::Write,
   92          _ => ToolCategory::Other,
   93      }
   94  }
   95
   96  fn extract_string_arg(input: &Value, keys: &[&str]) -> Option<String> {
   97      let obj = input.as_object()?;
   98      for k in keys {
   99          if let Some(s) = obj.get(*k).and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
  100              if !s.is_empty() {
  101                  return Some(s.to_string());
  102              }
  103          }
  104      }
  105      None
  106  }

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

Model & context

3 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L2factmodelContext

01 · Provider 以流式协议统一，工具调用必须完整再上送

SOURCE FACT

Provider trait 只要求实现 stream(model, system, messages, tools)；MessageStream 可以增量返回文本，但工具调用以完整对象返回。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

不同模型厂商先被翻译成同一种“消息水管”。普通文字可以一个词一个词流出，但工具参数不能半截就执行。

IMPLICATION

Provider 适配层承担格式差异，Agent 主循环不需要为每家 API 复制控制逻辑。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 281–282
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 283–286
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 021b0db8dbee · open ledger commit ↗
crates/goose-provider-types/src/base.rs:281–286PINNED ↗
  281  /// A message stream yields partial text content but complete tool calls, all within the Message object
  282  /// So a message with text will contain potentially just a word of a longer response, but tool calls
  283  /// messages will only be yielded once concatenated.
  284  pub type MessageStream = Pin<
  285      Box<dyn Stream<Item = Result<(Option<Message>, Option<ProviderUsage>), ProviderError>> + Send>,
  286  >;

  294  pub fn model_info_for_provider_model(provider_name: &str, model_name: &str) -> ModelInfo {
  295      let registry = CanonicalModelRegistry::bundled().ok();
  296      let canonical = registry.as_ref().and_then(|registry| {
  297          let canonical_id = map_to_canonical_model(provider_name, model_name, registry)?;
  298          let (provider, model) = canonical_id.split_once('/')?;
  299          registry.get(provider, model)
  300      });
  301
  302      let reasoning = canonical
  303          .as_ref()
  304          .and_then(|model| model.reasoning)
  305          .unwrap_or_else(|| ModelConfig::new(model_name).is_reasoning_model());
  306
  307      ModelInfo {
  308          name: model_name.to_string(),
  309          resolved_model: None,
  310          context_limit: ModelConfig::new(model_name)
  311              .with_canonical_limits(provider_name)
  312              .context_limit(),
  313          input_token_cost: None,
  314          output_token_cost: None,
  315          currency: None,
  316          supports_cache_control: None,
  317          reasoning,
  318      }
  319  }
  320
  321  /// Collect all chunks from a MessageStream into a single Message and ProviderUsage
  322  pub async fn collect_stream(
  323      mut stream: MessageStream,
  324  ) -> Result<(Message, ProviderUsage), ProviderError> {
  325      use futures::StreamExt;
  326
  327      let mut final_message: Option<Message> = None;
  328      let mut final_usage: Option<ProviderUsage> = None;
  329
  330      while let Some(result) = stream.next().await {
  331          let (msg_opt, usage_opt) = result?;
  332
  333          if let Some(msg) = msg_opt {
  334              final_message = Some(match final_message {
  335                  Some(mut prev) => {
  336                      for new_content in msg.content {
  337                          match (&mut prev.content.last_mut(), &new_content) {
  338                              // Coalesce consecutive text blocks
  339                              (
  340                                  Some(MessageContentBlock::Text(last_text)),
  341                                  MessageContentBlock::Text(new_text),
  342                              ) if last_text
  343                                  .annotations
  344                                  .as_ref()
  345                                  .and_then(|a| a.audience.as_ref())
  346                                  == new_text
  347                                      .annotations
  348                                      .as_ref()
  349                                      .and_then(|a| a.audience.as_ref()) =>
  350                              {
  351                                  last_text.text.push_str(&new_text.text);
  352                              }
  353                              _ => {
  354                                  prev.content.push(new_content);
  355                              }
  356                          }
  357                      }

  341          currency: None,

  343          reasoning,
  344          thinking_preservation_format: None,
  345          request_params: None,

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 80% 阈值触发结构化压缩

SOURCE FACT

当 Agent 自管上下文且估算 token/上下文上限大于阈值（默认 0.8）时触发压缩；压缩保留最新用户文本，并插入结构化摘要与继续指令。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

快塞满模型记忆时，它不会粗暴删掉全部历史，而是把旧进展整理成一张交接单，再把用户最新要求放回去。

IMPLICATION

压缩的目标不是“越短越好”，而是保留任务状态、文件改动、错误、待办和下一步。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 26–36
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 37–49
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   67  /// Compact messages by summarizing them
   68  ///
   69  /// This function performs the actual compaction by summarizing messages and updating
   70  /// their visibility metadata. It does not check thresholds - use `check_if_compaction_needed`
   71  /// first to determine if compaction is necessary.
   72  ///
   73  /// # Arguments
   74  /// * `provider` - The provider to use for summarization
   75  /// * `session_id` - The session to use for summarization
   76  /// * `conversation` - The current conversation history
   77  /// * `manual_compact` - If true, this is a manual compaction (don't preserve user message)
   78  pub async fn compact_messages(
   79      provider: &dyn Provider,
   80      model_config: &ModelConfig,
   81      session_id: &str,
   82      conversation: &Conversation,
   83      manual_compact: bool,
   84  ) -> Result<CompactionResult> {
   85      info!("Performing message compaction");
   86
   87      let messages = conversation.messages();
   88
   89      let has_text_only = |msg: &Message| {
   90          let has_text = msg
   91              .content
   92              .iter()
   93              .any(|c| matches!(c, MessageContent::Text(_)));
   94          let has_tool_content = msg.content.iter().any(|c| {
   95              matches!(
   96                  c,
   97                  MessageContent::ToolRequest(_) | MessageContent::ToolResponse(_)
   98              )
   99          });
  100          has_text && !has_tool_content
  101      };
  102
  103      // Find and preserve the most recent user message for non-manual compacts
  104      let (preserved_user_message, is_most_recent) = if !manual_compact {
  105          let found_msg = messages.iter().enumerate().rev().find_map(|(idx, msg)| {
  106              if !msg.is_agent_visible() || !matches!(msg.role, rmcp::model::Role::User) {
  107                  return None;
  108              }
  109
  110              let projected = msg.agent_visible_content();
  111              if !has_text_only(&projected) {
  112                  return None;
  113              }
  114
  115              let preserved = projected
  116                  .content
  117                  .into_iter()
  118                  .filter(|content| matches!(content, MessageContent::Text(_)))
  119                  .fold(
  120                      Message::user().with_metadata(MessageMetadata::agent_only()),

   48  pub struct CompactionResult {
   49      pub conversation: Conversation,
   50      /// Billable usage of the summarization call, counting the raw model
   51      /// output even when it is rewritten to the rendered structured summary.
   52      pub usage: ProviderUsage,
   53      /// Estimated tokens of the agent-visible context retained after

   55      /// rewritten to the rendered structured summary.

   58
   59  /// Compact messages by summarizing them
   60  ///
   61  /// This function performs the actual compaction by summarizing messages and updating
   62  /// their visibility metadata. It does not check thresholds - use `check_if_compaction_needed`
   63  /// first to determine if compaction is necessary.
   64  ///
   65  /// # Arguments
   66  /// * `provider` - The provider to use for summarization
   67  /// * `session_id` - The session to use for summarization
   68  /// * `conversation` - The current conversation history
   69  /// * `manual_compact` - If true, this is a manual compaction (don't preserve user message)
   70  pub async fn compact_messages(
   71      provider: &dyn Provider,
   72      model_config: &ModelConfig,
   73      session_id: &str,
   74      conversation: &Conversation,
   75      manual_compact: bool,
   76  ) -> Result<CompactionResult> {
   77      info!("Performing message compaction");
   78
   79      let messages = conversation.messages();
   80
   81      let has_text_only = |msg: &Message| {
   82          let has_text = msg
   83              .content
   84              .iter()
   85              .any(|c| matches!(c, MessageContent::Text(_)));
   86          let has_tool_content = msg.content.iter().any(|c| {
   87              matches!(
   88                  c,
   89                  MessageContent::ToolRequest(_) | MessageContent::ToolResponse(_)
   90              )
   91          });
   92          has_text && !has_tool_content
   93      };
   94
   95      // Turn-context events are agent-appended, never the message to preserve.
   96      let (preserved_user_message, preserved_idx, is_most_recent) = if !manual_compact {
   97          let found_msg = messages.iter().enumerate().rev().find_map(|(idx, msg)| {
   98              if !msg.is_agent_visible()
   99                  || msg.is_turn_context()
  100                  || !matches!(msg.role, rmcp::model::Role::User)
  101              {
  102                  return None;
  103              }
  104
  105              let projected = msg.agent_visible_content();
  106              if !has_text_only(&projected) {
  107                  return None;
  108              }
  109
  110              let preserved = projected
  111                  .content
  112                  .into_iter()
  113                  .filter(|content| matches!(content, MessageContent::Text(_)))
  114                  .fold(
  115                      Message::user().with_metadata(MessageMetadata::agent_only()),
  116                      Message::with_content,
  117                  );
  118              Some((idx, preserved))
  119          });

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 上下文超限采用渐进式工具结果剥离和一次恢复重试

SOURCE FACT

压缩会依次尝试移除 0%、10%、20%、50%、100% 的中间工具响应；Provider 明确报上下文超限时执行恢复压缩，第二次仍超限则终止。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

先保留尽可能多的工具证据，实在放不下才逐级清掉旧工具输出；不能靠无限压缩掩盖超限。

IMPLICATION

Harness 应区分账单 token、保留 token 和可牺牲的工具噪声，并给恢复动作设次数上限。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 319–357
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 358–398
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 021b0db8dbee · open ledger commit ↗
crates/goose/src/context_mgmt/mod.rs:319–398PINNED ↗
  319  async fn do_compact(
  320      provider: &dyn Provider,
  321      model_config: &ModelConfig,
  322      session_id: &str,
  323      messages: &[Message],
  324  ) -> Result<(Message, ProviderUsage), anyhow::Error> {
  325      let agent_visible_messages =
  326          Conversation::new_unvalidated(messages.iter().cloned()).agent_visible_messages();
  327
  328      // Try progressively removing more tool response messages from the middle to reduce context length
  329      let removal_percentages = [0, 10, 20, 50, 100];
  330
  331      for (attempt, &remove_percent) in removal_percentages.iter().enumerate() {
  332          let filtered_messages = filter_tool_responses(&agent_visible_messages, remove_percent);
  333
  334          let messages_text = filtered_messages
  335              .iter()
  336              .map(|&msg| format_message_for_compacting(msg))
  337              .collect::<Vec<_>>()
  338              .join("\n");
  339
  340          let context = SummarizeContext {
  341              messages: messages_text,
  342          };
  343
  344          let system_prompt = render_template("compaction.md", &context)?;
  345
  346          let user_message = Message::user()
  347              .with_text("Please summarize the conversation history provided in the system prompt.");
  348          let summarization_request = vec![user_message];
  349
  350          match crate::model_config::complete_fast(
  351              provider,
  352              model_config,
  353              session_id,
  354              &system_prompt,
  355              &summarization_request,
  356              &[],
  357          )
  358          .await
  359          {
  360              Ok((mut response, mut provider_usage)) => {
  361                  response.role = Role::User;
  362
  363                  // Usage must reflect the raw model output (billable tokens),
  364                  // so estimate before the response is rewritten to the smaller
  365                  // rendered summary.
  366                  crate::providers::usage_estimator::ensure_usage_tokens(
  367                      &mut provider_usage,
  368                      &system_prompt,
  369                      &summarization_request,
  370                      &response,
  371                      &[],
  372                  )
  373                  .await
  374                  .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("Failed to ensure usage tokens: {}", e))?;
  375
  376                  apply_structured_summary(&mut response);
  377
  378                  return Ok((response, provider_usage));
  379              }
  380              Err(e) => {
  381                  if matches!(e, ProviderError::ContextLengthExceeded(_)) {
  382                      if attempt < removal_percentages.len() - 1 {
  383                          continue;
  384                      } else {
  385                          return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  386                              "Failed to compact: context limit exceeded even after removing all tool responses"
  387                          ));
  388                      }
  389                  }
  390                  return Err(e.into());
  391              }
  392          }
  393      }
  394
  395      Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  396          "Unexpected: exhausted all attempts without returning"
  397      ))
  398  }
  399
  400  /// When the model didn't follow the structured output format (schema-ignoring
  401  /// models, user-customized prompts), the raw response text is kept unchanged
  402  /// as the summary.
  403  fn apply_structured_summary(response: &mut Message) {
  404      let Some(summary) = StructuredSummary::parse(&response.as_concat_text()) else {
  405          return;
  406      };
  407      match summary.render() {
  408          Ok(rendered) if !rendered.trim().is_empty() => {
  409              response.content = vec![MessageContent::text(rendered)];
  410          }
  411          Ok(_) => warn!(
  412              "Structured compaction summary rendered empty (broken template override?), keeping raw output"
  413          ),
  414          Err(e) => warn!(
  415              "Failed to render structured compaction summary, keeping raw output: {}",
  416              e
  417          ),
  418      }
  419  }
  420
  421  pub fn format_message_for_compacting(msg: &Message) -> String {
  422      let content_parts: Vec<String> = msg
  423          .content
  424          .iter()
  425          .filter_map(|content| match content {
  426              MessageContent::Text(text) => Some(text.text.clone()),
  427              MessageContent::Image(img) => Some(format!("[image: {}]", img.mime_type)),
  428              MessageContent::ToolRequest(req) => {
  429                  if let Ok(call) = &req.tool_call {
  430                      Some(format!(
  431                          "tool_request({}): {}",
  432                          call.name,
  433                          serde_json::to_string(&call.arguments)
  434                              .unwrap_or_else(|_| "<<invalid json>>".to_string())
  435                      ))
  436                  } else {
  437                      Some("tool_request: [error]".to_string())
  438                  }
  439              }
  440              MessageContent::ToolResponse(res) => {
  441                  if let Ok(result) = &res.tool_result {
  442                      let text_items: Vec<String> = result
  443                          .content
  444                          .iter()
  445                          .filter_map(|content| {
  446                              content.as_text().map(|text_str| text_str.text.clone())
  447                          })
  448                          .collect();
  449
  450                      if !text_items.is_empty() {
  451                          Some(format!("tool_response: {}", text_items.join("\n")))
  452                      } else {
  453                          Some("tool_response: [non-text content]".to_string())
  454                      }
  455                  } else {
  456                      Some("tool_response: [error]".to_string())
  457                  }
  458              }
  459              MessageContent::ToolConfirmationRequest(req) => {
  460                  Some(format!("tool_confirmation_request: {}", req.tool_name))
  461              }
  462              MessageContent::ActionRequired(action) => match &action.data {

  336  async fn do_compact(
  337      provider: &dyn Provider,
  338      model_config: &ModelConfig,
  339      session_id: &str,
  340      messages: &[Message],
  341  ) -> Result<(Message, ProviderUsage), anyhow::Error> {
  342      // Keep stale per-turn state out of the summary.
  343      let agent_visible_messages = Conversation::new_unvalidated(
  344          messages
  345              .iter()
  346              .filter(|msg| !msg.is_turn_context())
  347              .cloned(),
  348      )
  349      .agent_visible_messages();
  350
  351      let model = FastCompactionModel {
  352          provider,
  353          model_config,
  354          session_id,
  355      };
  356      let summary = goose_context_management::summarize(
  357          &model,
  358          Some(&GooseTokenEstimator),
  359          &compaction_templates()?,
  360          &agent_visible_messages,
  361      )
  362      .await?;
  363
  364      Ok((summary.message, summary.usage))
  365  }

  367  pub use goose_context_management::format_message_for_compacting;

  369  pub fn compute_tool_call_cutoff(context_limit: usize, compaction_threshold: f64) -> usize {
  370      let threshold = if compaction_threshold > 0.0 && compaction_threshold <= 1.0 {
  371          compaction_threshold
  372      } else {
  373          DEFAULT_COMPACTION_THRESHOLD
  374      };
  375      let effective_limit = (context_limit as f64 * threshold) as usize;
  376      (3 * effective_limit / 20_000).clamp(10, 500)
  377  }
  378
  379  pub fn tool_ids_to_summarize(
  380      conversation: &Conversation,
  381      cutoff: usize,
  382      protect_last_n: usize,
  383  ) -> Vec<String> {
  384      let messages = conversation.messages();
  385
  386      let mut tool_call_ids: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
  387
  388      for msg in messages.iter() {
  389          if !msg.is_agent_visible() {
  390              continue;
  391          }
  392
  393          for content in &msg.content {
  394              if let MessageContent::ToolRequest(req) = content {
  395                  tool_call_ids.push(req.id.clone());
  396              }
  397          }
  398      }
  399
  400      // Never summarize the last N tool calls (current turn)
  401      let eligible = tool_call_ids.len().saturating_sub(protect_last_n);
  402      if eligible <= cutoff + TOOLCALL_SUMMARIZATION_BATCH_SIZE {
  403          return Vec::new();
  404      }
  405
  406      tool_call_ids
  407          .into_iter()
  408          .take(TOOLCALL_SUMMARIZATION_BATCH_SIZE)
  409          .collect()
  410  }
  411
  412  fn agent_visible_tool_pair(conversation: &Conversation, tool_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<Message>> {
  413      let matching_messages = conversation
  414          .messages()
  415          .iter()
  416          .filter(|m| {
  417              m.content.iter().any(|c| match c {
  418                  MessageContent::ToolRequest(req) => req.id == tool_id,
  419                  MessageContent::ToolResponse(resp) => resp.id == tool_id,
  420                  _ => false,
  421              })
  422          })
  423          .cloned()
  424          .collect::<Vec<_>>();
  425      let matching_messages =
  426          Conversation::new_unvalidated(matching_messages).agent_visible_messages();
  427
  428      let has_request = matching_messages.iter().any(|message| {
  429          message.content.iter().any(
  430              |content| matches!(content, MessageContent::ToolRequest(request) if request.id == tool_id),
  431          )
  432      });
  433      let has_response = matching_messages.iter().any(|message| {
  434          message.content.iter().any(
  435              |content| matches!(content, MessageContent::ToolResponse(response) if response.id == tool_id),
  436          )
  437      });
  438      if !has_request || !has_response {
  439          return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  440              "No agent-visible tool pair found for tool id: {}",
  441              tool_id
  442          ));
  443      }
  444      Ok(matching_messages)
  445  }
  446
  447  pub async fn summarize_tool_call(
  448      provider: &dyn Provider,
  449      model_config: &ModelConfig,
  450      session_id: &str,
  451      conversation: &Conversation,
  452      tool_id: &str,
  453  ) -> Result<Message> {
  454      let matching_messages = agent_visible_tool_pair(conversation, tool_id)?;
  455
  456      let formatted = matching_messages
  457          .iter()
  458          .map(format_message_for_compacting)
  459          .collect::<Vec<_>>()
  460          .join("\n");
  461
  462      let user_message = Message::user().with_text(formatted);
  463      let summarization_request = vec![user_message];
  464
  465      let system_prompt = indoc! {r#"
  466                  Your task is to summarize a tool call & response pair to save tokens.
  467
  468                  Reply with a single message that describes what happened. Typically a tool call
  469                  asks for something using a bunch of parameters and then the result is also some

DIMENSION · EXECUTION

Execution & sandbox

1 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1limitationexecution

01 · 内置开发者工具没有强制工作区边界

SOURCE FACT

shell 工具最终启动系统 bash/sh/PowerShell；Flatpak 下使用 flatpak-spawn --host。edit 工具解析并接受绝对路径，随后直接 fs::write。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

Goose 会先决定“该不该执行”，但一旦放行，命令通常是在真实电脑环境里跑，不是在一个只能碰项目目录的小盒子里。

IMPLICATION

Goose 的默认安全核心是策略检查与人类审批，不是内核级隔离；部署到高风险环境应再叠加容器、VM 或受限执行器。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 25–36
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 37–49
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

CAVEAT / RISK
  • 外层桌面打包、企业运行平台或用户自行容器化可能增加隔离；本结论针对仓库内置 developer shell/edit 的实现。

   58  #[cfg(not(windows))]
   59  fn unix_shell_flavor(shell: &str) -> UnixShellFlavor {
   60      let name = std::path::Path::new(shell)
   61          .file_stem()
   62          .and_then(|stem| stem.to_str())
   63          .unwrap_or(shell)
   64          .to_ascii_lowercase();
   65
   66      match name.as_str() {
   67          "nu" | "nushell" => UnixShellFlavor::Nushell,
   68          _ => UnixShellFlavor::Posix,
   69      }
   70  }

   75          UnixShellFlavor::Nushell => "print ($env.PATH | str join (char esep))",
   76          UnixShellFlavor::Posix => "echo $PATH",
   77      };
   78
   79      ["-l", "-i", "-c", probe]
   80  }

   88  ///

DIMENSION · TOOLS

Tool registration & execution

1 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1facttools

01 · 同一模型 turn 的多个工具经检查后并发执行

SOURCE FACT

工具请求先统一通过 inspector 和许可分组；已批准及用户批准的工具流随后用 stream::select_all 合并，结果按 request_id 配回各自响应。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

模型一次要读三个文件时，不必傻等第一个完成再做第二个；但每个调用先过安全和审批门。

IMPLICATION

并发提高吞吐，但必须用稳定 ID 做结果配对，并允许单个工具发通知、请求交互或取消。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 2210–2236
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 2237–2265
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

Connectors, plugins & MCP

3 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 工具被统一命名空间化、缓存和动态刷新

SOURCE FACT

ExtensionManager 从所有客户端分页拉取工具，按 extension__tool 前缀公开（特殊配置可不加前缀），缓存聚合结果；扩展增删会提升版本并失效缓存。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

各插件都可能有一个叫 search 的工具，所以 Goose 默认把它们改成“插件名__search”，避免撞名，并缓存工具清单减少重复查询。

IMPLICATION

工具注册表既是性能组件也是一致性组件；热插拔后必须原子失效。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1271–1305
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 1306–1342
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

 1290      fn filter_tools(
 1291          &self,
 1292          tools: &[Tool],
 1293          extension_name: Option<&str>,
 1294          exclude: Option<&str>,
 1295      ) -> Vec<Tool> {
 1296          let extension_name_normalized = extension_name.map(name_to_key);
 1297          let exclude_normalized = exclude.map(name_to_key);
 1298
 1299          tools
 1300              .iter()
 1301              .filter(|tool| {
 1302                  let tool_owner = get_tool_owner(tool)
 1303                      .map(|s| name_to_key(&s))
 1304                      .unwrap_or_else(|| tool.name.split("__").next().unwrap_or("").to_string());
 1305
 1306                  if let Some(ref excluded) = exclude_normalized {
 1307                      if tool_owner == *excluded {
 1308                          return false;
 1309                      }
 1310                  }
 1311
 1312                  if let Some(ref name_filter) = extension_name_normalized {
 1313                      tool_owner == *name_filter
 1314                  } else {
 1315                      true
 1316                  }
 1317              })
 1318              .cloned()
 1319              .collect()
 1320      }

 1323          {
 1324              let cache = self.tools_cache.lock().await;
 1325              if let Some(ref tools) = *cache {
 1326                  return Ok(Arc::clone(tools));
 1327              }
 1328          }
 1329
 1330          let version_before = self.tools_cache_version.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
 1331          let tools = Arc::new(self.fetch_all_tools(session_id).await?);
 1332
 1333          {
 1334              let mut cache = self.tools_cache.lock().await;
 1335              let version_after = self.tools_cache_version.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
 1336              if version_after == version_before && cache.is_none() {
 1337                  *cache = Some(Arc::clone(&tools));
 1338              }
 1339          }
 1340
 1341          Ok(tools)
 1342      }

L1factconnectors

02 · 远端 MCP 支持 HTTP、OAuth 和交互通知

SOURCE FACT

扩展可通过 streamable HTTP（Unix 上还可走 socket）连接，复用凭证存储和 OAuth；工具执行可同时返回通知流、ActionRequired 流与最终结果。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

插件不一定是本机子进程，也可以是要登录的远程服务；执行过程中还能弹出“需要用户操作”的中间事件。

IMPLICATION

Connector 模型必须同时处理认证生命周期、取消、通知和最终值，不能只把工具当普通函数。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 612–670
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 671–730
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

crates/goose/src/agents/extension_manager.rs:612–730PINNED ↗
  612  async fn connect_with_auth(
  613      auth_manager: rmcp::transport::AuthorizationManager,
  614      uri: &str,
  615      timeout: Duration,
  616      headers: &HashMap<String, String>,
  617      provider: SharedProvider,
  618      client_name: String,
  619      capabilities: GooseMcpClientCapabilities,
  620      roots_dir: &std::path::Path,
  621  ) -> ExtensionResult<Box<dyn McpClientTrait>> {
  622      let mut auth_headers = HeaderMap::new();
  623      auth_headers.insert(reqwest::header::USER_AGENT, GOOSE_USER_AGENT);
  624      for (key, value) in headers {
  625          auth_headers.insert(
  626              HeaderName::try_from(key)
  627                  .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header: {}", key)))?,
  628              value.parse().map_err(|_| {
  629                  ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header value: {}", key))
  630              })?,
  631          );
  632      }
  633      #[allow(unused_mut)]
  634      let mut auth_client_builder = reqwest::Client::builder().default_headers(auth_headers);
  635      #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
  636      {
  637          auth_client_builder = auth_client_builder.tcp_user_timeout(Some(timeout));
  638      }
  639      let auth_http_client = auth_client_builder
  640          .build()
  641          .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError("could not construct http client".to_string()))?;
  642      let auth_client = AuthClient::new(auth_http_client, auth_manager);
  643      let transport = StreamableHttpClientTransport::with_client(
  644          auth_client,
  645          StreamableHttpClientTransportConfig::with_uri(uri),
  646      );
  647      Ok(Box::new(
  648          McpClient::connect(
  649              transport,
  650              timeout,
  651              provider,
  652              client_name,
  653              capabilities,
  654              roots_dir.to_path_buf(),
  655          )
  656          .await?,
  657      ))
  658  }
  659
  660  #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  661  async fn create_streamable_http_client(
  662      uri: &str,
  663      timeout: Option<u64>,
  664      headers: &HashMap<String, String>,
  665      name: &str,
  666      socket: Option<&str>,
  667      credential_store: Box<dyn CredentialStore>,
  668      provider: SharedProvider,
  669      client_name: String,
  670      capabilities: GooseMcpClientCapabilities,
  671      roots_dir: &std::path::Path,
  672  ) -> ExtensionResult<Box<dyn McpClientTrait>> {
  673      #[cfg(unix)]
  674      if let Some(socket_path) = socket {
  675          return create_unix_socket_http_client(
  676              uri,
  677              timeout,
  678              headers,
  679              name,
  680              socket_path,
  681              provider,
  682              client_name,
  683              capabilities,
  684              roots_dir,
  685          )
  686          .await;
  687      }
  688      #[cfg(not(unix))]
  689      if socket.is_some() {
  690          return Err(ExtensionError::ConfigError(
  691              "Unix domain socket transport is not supported on this platform".to_string(),
  692          ));
  693      }
  694
  695      let mut default_headers = HeaderMap::new();
  696
  697      default_headers.insert(reqwest::header::USER_AGENT, GOOSE_USER_AGENT);
  698
  699      for (key, value) in headers {
  700          default_headers.insert(
  701              HeaderName::try_from(key)
  702                  .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header: {}", key)))?,
  703              value.parse().map_err(|_| {
  704                  ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header value: {}", key))
  705              })?,
  706          );
  707      }
  708
  709      let timeout_duration = Duration::from_secs(resolve_timeout(timeout));
  710
  711      #[allow(unused_mut)]
  712      let mut http_client_builder = reqwest::Client::builder().default_headers(default_headers);
  713      #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
  714      {
  715          http_client_builder = http_client_builder.tcp_user_timeout(Some(timeout_duration));
  716      }
  717      let http_client = http_client_builder
  718          .build()
  719          .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError("could not construct http client".to_string()))?;
  720
  721      let transport = StreamableHttpClientTransport::with_client(
  722          http_client,
  723          StreamableHttpClientTransportConfig::with_uri(uri),
  724      );
  725
  726      // If we have stored OAuth credentials, try refreshing and connecting directly.
  727      // This avoids the unnecessary 401 → browser re-auth cycle on every new session.
  728      if credential_store.load().await.is_ok_and(|c| c.is_some()) {
  729          match oauth_flow(&uri.to_string(), &name.to_string()).await {
  730              Ok(auth_manager) => {
  731                  let auth_result = connect_with_auth(
  732                      auth_manager,
  733                      uri,
  734                      timeout_duration,
  735                      headers,
  736                      provider.clone(),
  737                      client_name.clone(),
  738                      capabilities.clone(),
  739                      roots_dir,
  740                  )
  741                  .await;
  742
  743                  if let Err(error) = &auth_result {
  744                      if clear_credentials_on_post_refresh_auth_failure(
  745                          credential_store.as_ref(),
  746                          name,
  747                          error,
  748                      )
  749                      .await
  750                      {
  751                          warn!(
  752                              "[OAuth:{}] Refreshed token was rejected, falling back to browser auth",
  753                              name
  754                          );
  755                      } else {
  756                          return auth_result;
  757                      }
  758                  } else {
  759                      return auth_result;
  760                  }
  761              }
  762              Err(e) => {
  763                  warn!(
  764                      "[OAuth:{}] Proactive refresh failed: {}, falling back to unauthenticated attempt",
  765                      name, e
  766                  );
  767              }
  768          }
  769      }
  770
  771      let client_res = McpClient::connect(
  772          transport,
  773          timeout_duration,
  774          provider.clone(),
  775          client_name.clone(),
  776          capabilities.clone(),
  777          roots_dir.to_path_buf(),
  778      )
  779      .await;
  780
  781      if should_attempt_oauth_fallback(&client_res) {
  782          match oauth_flow(&uri.to_string(), &name.to_string()).await {
  783              Ok(auth_manager) => {
  784                  connect_with_auth(
  785                      auth_manager,
  786                      uri,
  787                      timeout_duration,
  788                      headers,
  789                      provider,
  790                      client_name,
  791                      capabilities,
  792                      roots_dir,
  793                  )
  794                  .await
  795              }
  796              Err(_) => Ok(Box::new(client_res?)),
  797          }
  798      } else {
  799          Ok(Box::new(client_res?))
  800      }
  801  }

  619  const GOOSE_USER_AGENT: reqwest::header::HeaderValue =
  620      reqwest::header::HeaderValue::from_static(concat!("goose/", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")));

  622  #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  623  async fn connect_with_auth(
  624      auth_manager: rmcp::transport::AuthorizationManager,
  625      action_required: Arc<ActionRequiredManager>,
  626      uri: &str,
  627      timeout: Duration,
  628      headers: &HashMap<String, String>,
  629      provider: SharedProvider,
  630      client_name: String,
  631      capabilities: GooseMcpClientCapabilities,
  632      roots_dir: &std::path::Path,
  633      extension_manager: Weak<ExtensionManager>,
  634  ) -> ExtensionResult<Box<dyn McpClientTrait>> {
  635      let mut auth_headers = HeaderMap::new();
  636      auth_headers.insert(reqwest::header::USER_AGENT, GOOSE_USER_AGENT);
  637      for (key, value) in headers {
  638          auth_headers.insert(
  639              HeaderName::try_from(key)
  640                  .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header: {}", key)))?,
  641              value.parse().map_err(|_| {
  642                  ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header value: {}", key))
  643              })?,
  644          );
  645      }
  646      #[allow(unused_mut)]
  647      let mut auth_client_builder = reqwest::Client::builder().default_headers(auth_headers);
  648      #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
  649      {
  650          auth_client_builder = auth_client_builder.tcp_user_timeout(Some(timeout));
  651      }
  652      let auth_http_client = auth_client_builder
  653          .build()
  654          .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError("could not construct http client".to_string()))?;
  655      let auth_client = AuthClient::new(auth_http_client, auth_manager);
  656      let transport = StreamableHttpClientTransport::with_client(
  657          auth_client,
  658          StreamableHttpClientTransportConfig::with_uri(uri),
  659      );
  660      Ok(Box::new(
  661          McpClient::connect(
  662              transport,
  663              timeout,
  664              provider,
  665              client_name,
  666              capabilities,
  667              roots_dir.to_path_buf(),
  668              action_required,
  669              extension_manager,
  670          )
  671          .await?,
  672      ))
  673  }

  711          ));
  712      }

  721                  .map_err(|_| ExtensionError::ConfigError(format!("invalid header: {}", key)))?,

  724              })?,
  725          );

  775                          );

  779                  } else {
  780                      return auth_result;
  781                  }
  782              }
  783              Err(e) => {
  784                  warn!(
  785                      "[OAuth:{}] Proactive refresh failed: {}, falling back to unauthenticated attempt",
  786                      name, e
  787                  );
  788              }
  789          }
  790      }
  791
  792      let client_res = McpClient::connect(
  793          transport,
  794          timeout_duration,
  795          provider.clone(),
  796          client_name.clone(),
  797          capabilities.clone(),
  798          roots_dir.to_path_buf(),
  799          action_required.clone(),
  800          extension_manager.clone(),
  801      )
03
L1factconnectors

03 · MCP UI 元数据采用“先剥离、再可信补写”

SOURCE FACT

客户端返回的 goose.mcpApp 和内部工具更新元数据会先被删除；只有宿主主动读取关联资源后才插入可信附件。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

插件不能只靠自己声称“这是可信 UI”就让前端照单全收，Goose 会清掉敏感标记并由宿主重新验证、重新封装。

IMPLICATION

所有能改变宿主 UI/行为的扩展元数据都应有信任边界。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 322–333
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 334–346
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 021b0db8dbee · open ledger commit ↗
crates/goose/src/agents/extension_manager.rs:322–346PINNED ↗
  322  fn remove_untrusted_mcp_app_meta(result: &mut CallToolResult) {
  323      let Some(meta) = result.meta.as_mut() else {
  324          return;
  325      };
  326
  327      meta.0.remove(TRUSTED_TOOL_UPDATE_META_KEY);
  328
  329      let remove_goose = meta
  330          .0
  331          .get_mut("goose")
  332          .and_then(Value::as_object_mut)
  333          .map(|goose_meta| {
  334              goose_meta.remove("mcpApp");
  335              goose_meta.is_empty()
  336          })
  337          .unwrap_or(false);
  338
  339      if remove_goose {
  340          meta.0.remove("goose");
  341      }
  342
  343      if meta.0.is_empty() {
  344          result.meta = None;
  345      }
  346  }
  347
  348  fn insert_trusted_tool_update_meta(
  349      result: &mut CallToolResult,
  350      attachment: &GooseMcpAppToolAttachment,
  351  ) {
  352      let Ok(attachment_value) = serde_json::to_value(attachment) else {
  353          return;
  354      };
  355
  356      let mut meta_map = result
  357          .meta
  358          .as_ref()
  359          .map(|meta| meta.0.clone())
  360          .unwrap_or_default();
  361      let mut trusted_meta = serde_json::Map::new();
  362      trusted_meta.insert("mcpApp".to_string(), attachment_value);
  363      meta_map.insert(
  364          TRUSTED_TOOL_UPDATE_META_KEY.to_string(),
  365          Value::Object(trusted_meta),
  366      );
  367      result.meta = Some(Meta(meta_map));
  368  }
  369
  370  fn is_unprefixed_extension(config: &ExtensionConfig) -> bool {
  371      match config {
  372          ExtensionConfig::Platform { name, .. } | ExtensionConfig::Builtin { name, .. } => {
  373              PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  374                  .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  375                  .is_some_and(|def| def.unprefixed_tools)
  376          }
  377          _ => false,
  378      }
  379  }
  380
  381  /// Returns true if the named extension is a first-class platform extension
  382  /// whose tools are exposed unprefixed and remain visible during code execution mode.
  383  pub fn is_first_class_extension(name: &str) -> bool {
  384      PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  385          .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  386          .is_some_and(|def| def.unprefixed_tools)
  387  }
  388
  389  pub fn is_hidden_extension(name: &str) -> bool {
  390      PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  391          .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  392          .is_some_and(|def| def.hidden)
  393  }

  330      let Some(meta) = result.meta.as_mut() else {
  331          return;
  332      };
  333
  334      meta.0.remove(TRUSTED_TOOL_UPDATE_META_KEY);
  335
  336      let remove_goose = meta
  337          .0
  338          .get_mut("goose")
  339          .and_then(Value::as_object_mut)
  340          .map(|goose_meta| {
  341              goose_meta.remove("mcpApp");
  342              goose_meta.is_empty()
  343          })
  344          .unwrap_or(false);
  345
  346      if remove_goose {
  347          meta.0.remove("goose");
  348      }
  349
  350      if meta.0.is_empty() {
  351          result.meta = None;
  352      }
  353  }
  354
  355  fn insert_trusted_tool_update_meta(
  356      result: &mut CallToolResult,
  357      attachment: &GooseMcpAppToolAttachment,
  358  ) {
  359      let Ok(attachment_value) = serde_json::to_value(attachment) else {
  360          return;
  361      };
  362
  363      let mut meta_map = result
  364          .meta
  365          .as_ref()
  366          .map(|meta| meta.0.clone())
  367          .unwrap_or_default();
  368      let mut trusted_meta = serde_json::Map::new();
  369      trusted_meta.insert("mcpApp".to_string(), attachment_value);
  370      meta_map.insert(
  371          TRUSTED_TOOL_UPDATE_META_KEY.to_string(),
  372          Value::Object(trusted_meta),
  373      );
  374      result.meta = Some(MetaObject(meta_map));
  375  }
  376
  377  fn is_unprefixed_extension(config: &ExtensionConfig) -> bool {
  378      match config {
  379          ExtensionConfig::Platform { name, .. } | ExtensionConfig::Builtin { name, .. } => {
  380              PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  381                  .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  382                  .is_some_and(|def| def.unprefixed_tools)
  383          }
  384          _ => false,
  385      }
  386  }
  387
  388  /// Returns true if the named extension is a first-class platform extension
  389  /// whose tools are exposed unprefixed and remain visible during code execution mode.
  390  pub fn is_first_class_extension(name: &str) -> bool {
  391      PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  392          .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  393          .is_some_and(|def| def.unprefixed_tools)
  394  }
  395
  396  pub fn is_hidden_extension(name: &str) -> bool {
  397      PLATFORM_EXTENSIONS
  398          .get(name_to_key(name).as_str())
  399          .is_some_and(|def| def.hidden)
  400  }

DIMENSION · SECURITY

Security, approval & permissions

2 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factsecurity

01 · 工具检查顺序体现“危险优先”

SOURCE FACT

检查器固定按 Security、Egress、Adversary、Permission、Repetition 顺序注册。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

先看是否像恶意命令和数据外传，再做额外对抗审查，然后才判断用户是否需要点批准，最后检查重复循环。

IMPLICATION

审批不是唯一防线；危险检测应在便利性策略之前执行。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 659–672
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 673–688
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

CAVEAT / RISK
  • 具体 inspector 是否生效还受配置控制；例如模式扫描可被关闭。

  659      /// Create a tool inspection manager with default inspectors
  660      fn create_tool_inspection_manager(
  661          permission_manager: Arc<PermissionManager>,
  662          provider: SharedProvider,
  663          session_manager: Arc<SessionManager>,
  664      ) -> ToolInspectionManager {
  665          let mut tool_inspection_manager = ToolInspectionManager::new();
  666
  667          // Add security inspector (highest priority - runs first)
  668          tool_inspection_manager.add_inspector(Box::new(SecurityInspector::new()));
  669          tool_inspection_manager.add_inspector(Box::new(EgressInspector::new()));
  670
  671          // Add adversary inspector (LLM-based review, enabled by ~/.config/goose/adversary.md)
  672          tool_inspection_manager.add_inspector(Box::new(AdversaryInspector::new(
  673              provider.clone(),
  674              session_manager.clone(),
  675          )));
  676
  677          // Add permission inspector (medium-high priority)
  678          tool_inspection_manager.add_inspector(Box::new(PermissionInspector::new(
  679              permission_manager,
  680              provider,
  681              session_manager,
  682          )));
  683
  684          // Add repetition inspector (lower priority - basic repetition checking)
  685          tool_inspection_manager.add_inspector(Box::new(RepetitionInspector::new(None)));
  686
  687          tool_inspection_manager
  688      }

02
L1factsecurity

02 · Auto、Approve、SmartApprove 是不同权限语义

SOURCE FACT

Auto 模式允许所有调用；Approve/SmartApprove 尊重显式许可，SmartApprove 还会依据工具注解或 LLM 判断只读调用是否可自动放行，未知调用默认要求审批。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

“智能批准”不是无条件执行：读文件一类操作可能自动过，写文件或判断不清的操作仍会问人。

IMPLICATION

权限模式必须在 UI 和审计记录中显式呈现，不能用一个笼统的“安全模式”标签。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 159–212
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 213–268
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  159                  let action = match goose_mode {
  160                      GooseMode::Chat => continue,
  161                      GooseMode::Auto => InspectionAction::Allow,
  162                      GooseMode::Approve | GooseMode::SmartApprove => {
  163                          // 1. Check user-defined permission first
  164                          if let Some(level) = permission_manager.get_user_permission(tool_name) {
  165                              match level {
  166                                  PermissionLevel::AlwaysAllow => InspectionAction::Allow,
  167                                  PermissionLevel::NeverAllow => InspectionAction::Deny,
  168                                  PermissionLevel::AskBefore => {
  169                                      InspectionAction::RequireApproval(None)
  170                                  }
  171                              }
  172                          // 2. Check for a read-only annotation in SmartApprove mode
  173                          } else if goose_mode == GooseMode::SmartApprove
  174                              && self.is_readonly_annotated_tool(tool_name)
  175                          {
  176                              InspectionAction::Allow
  177                          // 3. Special case for extension management
  178                          } else if tool_name == MANAGE_EXTENSIONS_TOOL_NAME_COMPLETE {
  179                              InspectionAction::RequireApproval(Some(
  180                                  "Extension management requires approval for security".to_string(),
  181                              ))
  182                          // 4. Defer to LLM detection (SmartApprove, uncached or legacy cached allow)
  183                          } else if goose_mode == GooseMode::SmartApprove
  184                              && matches!(
  185                                  permission_manager.get_smart_approve_permission(tool_name),
  186                                  None | Some(PermissionLevel::AlwaysAllow)
  187                              )
  188                          {
  189                              llm_detect_candidates.push(request);
  190                              continue;
  191                          // 5. Default: require approval for unknown tools
  192                          } else {
  193                              InspectionAction::RequireApproval(None)
  194                          }
  195                      }
  196                  };
  197
  198                  let reason = match &action {
  199                      InspectionAction::Allow => {
  200                          if goose_mode == GooseMode::Auto {
  201                              "Auto mode - all tools approved".to_string()
  202                          } else if self.is_readonly_annotated_tool(tool_name) {
  203                              "Tool annotated as read-only".to_string()
  204                          } else {
  205                              "User permission allows this tool".to_string()
  206                          }
  207                      }
  208                      InspectionAction::Deny => "User permission denies this tool".to_string(),
  209                      InspectionAction::RequireApproval(_) => {
  210                          if tool_name == MANAGE_EXTENSIONS_TOOL_NAME_COMPLETE {
  211                              "Extension management requires user approval".to_string()
  212                          } else {
  213                              "Tool requires user approval".to_string()
  214                          }
  215                      }
  216                  };
  217
  218                  results.push(InspectionResult {
  219                      tool_request_id: request.id.clone(),
  220                      action,
  221                      reason,
  222                      confidence: 1.0, // Permission decisions are definitive
  223                      inspector_name: self.name().to_string(),
  224                      finding_id: None,
  225                  });
  226              }
  227          }
  228
  229          // LLM-based read-only detection for deferred SmartApprove candidates
  230          if !llm_detect_candidates.is_empty() {
  231              let detected_request_ids: HashSet<String> = match self.provider.lock().await.clone() {
  232                  Some(provider) => detect_read_only_requests(
  233                      provider,
  234                      &self.session_manager,
  235                      session_id,
  236                      llm_detect_candidates.to_vec(),
  237                  )
  238                  .await
  239                  .into_iter()
  240                  .collect(),
  241                  None => Default::default(),
  242              };
  243
  244              for candidate in &llm_detect_candidates {
  245                  let is_readonly = detected_request_ids.contains(&candidate.id);
  246
  247                  cache_non_readonly_decision(permission_manager, candidate, is_readonly);
  248
  249                  results.push(InspectionResult {
  250                      tool_request_id: candidate.id.clone(),
  251                      action: if is_readonly {
  252                          InspectionAction::Allow
  253                      } else {
  254                          InspectionAction::RequireApproval(None)
  255                      },
  256                      reason: if is_readonly {
  257                          "LLM detected as read-only".to_string()
  258                      } else {
  259                          "Tool requires user approval".to_string()
  260                      },
  261                      confidence: 1.0, // Permission decisions are definitive
  262                      inspector_name: self.name().to_string(),
  263                      finding_id: None,
  264                  });
  265              }
  266          }
  267
  268          Ok(results)
  269      }

01
L1factobservability

01 · 会话、消息、成本与压缩指标落到 SQLite/WAL

SOURCE FACT

SessionManager 使用 SQLite、WAL 和迁移管理持久化 sessions、messages、usage_ledger；会话记录 provider/model/mode/project/parent，usage ledger 区分输入输出、缓存、总成本与压缩后保留 token。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

对话不只是屏幕上的临时文本：每条消息、用的模型、父子会话、花费和压缩前后 token 都能落盘追踪。

IMPLICATION

成本与上下文恢复应成为一等数据模型，而不是散落在日志字符串里。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 45–69
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 70–96
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

02
L1factobservability

02 · Tracing 以 goose:: span 生成 trace/span 观测事件

SOURCE FACT

ObservationLayer 仅处理 target 以 goose:: 开头的 tracing 事件，跟踪 span 层级、输入输出和批次，并可把 reply 最终文本记录为 trace_output。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

内部关键步骤会形成一棵调用轨迹，不是只打一长串平面日志；同时避免把所有依赖库噪声都收进来。

IMPLICATION

观测层和业务层通过结构化 span 字段连接，适合接入 Langfuse 等后端，但敏感输出治理仍需部署方关注。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 103–142
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 143–184
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 021b0db8dbee · open ledger commit ↗
crates/goose/src/tracing/observation_layer.rs:103–184PINNED ↗
  103  impl ObservationLayer {
  104      pub async fn handle_span(&self, span_id: u64, span_data: SpanData) {
  105          let observation_id = span_data.observation_id.clone();
  106
  107          {
  108              let mut spans = self.span_tracker.lock().await;
  109              spans.add_span(span_id, observation_id.clone());
  110          }
  111
  112          // Get parent ID if it exists
  113          let parent_id = if let Some(parent_span_id) = span_data.parent_span_id {
  114              let spans = self.span_tracker.lock().await;
  115              spans.get_span(parent_span_id).cloned()
  116          } else {
  117              None
  118          };
  119
  120          let trace_id = self.ensure_trace_id().await;
  121
  122          // Create the span observation
  123          let mut batch = self.batch_manager.lock().await;
  124          batch.add_event(
  125              "observation-create",
  126              json!({
  127                  "id": observation_id,
  128                  "traceId": trace_id,
  129                  "type": "SPAN",
  130                  "name": span_data.name,
  131                  "startTime": span_data.start_time,
  132                  "parentObservationId": parent_id,
  133                  "metadata": span_data.metadata,
  134                  "level": span_data.level
  135              }),
  136          );
  137      }
  138
  139      pub async fn handle_span_close(&self, span_id: u64) {
  140          let observation_id = {
  141              let mut spans = self.span_tracker.lock().await;
  142              spans.remove_span(span_id)
  143          };
  144
  145          if let Some(observation_id) = observation_id {
  146              let trace_id = self.ensure_trace_id().await;
  147              let mut batch = self.batch_manager.lock().await;
  148              batch.add_event(
  149                  "observation-update",
  150                  json!({
  151                      "id": observation_id,
  152                      "type": "SPAN",
  153                      "traceId": trace_id,
  154                      "endTime": Utc::now().to_rfc3339()
  155                  }),
  156              );
  157          }
  158      }
  159
  160      pub async fn ensure_trace_id(&self) -> String {
  161          let mut spans = self.span_tracker.lock().await;
  162          if let Some(id) = spans.current_trace_id.clone() {
  163              return id;
  164          }
  165
  166          let trace_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
  167          spans.current_trace_id = Some(trace_id.clone());
  168
  169          let mut batch = self.batch_manager.lock().await;
  170          batch.add_event(
  171              "trace-create",
  172              json!({
  173                  "id": trace_id,
  174                  "name": Utc::now().timestamp().to_string(),
  175                  "timestamp": Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
  176                  "input": {},
  177                  "metadata": {},
  178                  "tags": [],
  179                  "public": false
  180              }),
  181          );
  182
  183          trace_id
  184      }
  185
  186      pub async fn update_trace(&self, updates: serde_json::Map<String, Value>) {
  187          let trace_id = self.ensure_trace_id().await;
  188          let mut body = json!({ "id": trace_id });
  189          for (k, v) in updates {
  190              body[k] = v;
  191          }
  192          let mut batch = self.batch_manager.lock().await;
  193          batch.add_event("trace-create", body);
  194      }
  195
  196      pub async fn handle_record(&self, span_id: u64, metadata: serde_json::Map<String, Value>) {
  197          // Handle trace-level fields by updating the trace itself
  198          let trace_fields: Vec<&str> = vec!["trace_input", "trace_output"];
  199          let has_trace_fields = trace_fields.iter().any(|f| metadata.contains_key(*f));
  200
  201          if has_trace_fields {
  202              let mut trace_updates = serde_json::Map::new();
  203              if let Some(val) = metadata.get("trace_input") {
  204                  trace_updates.insert("input".to_string(), val.clone());
  205              }
  206              if let Some(val) = metadata.get("trace_output") {
  207                  trace_updates.insert("output".to_string(), val.clone());
  208              }
  209              if !trace_updates.is_empty() {
  210                  self.update_trace(trace_updates).await;
  211              }
  212          }
  213
  214          // Filter out trace-level fields from span metadata
  215          let span_metadata: serde_json::Map<String, Value> = metadata
  216              .into_iter()
  217              .filter(|(k, _)| !trace_fields.contains(&k.as_str()))
  218              .collect();
  219
  220          if span_metadata.is_empty() {
  221              return;
  222          }
  223
  224          let observation_id = {
  225              let spans = self.span_tracker.lock().await;
  226              spans.get_span(span_id).cloned()
  227          };
  228
  229          if let Some(observation_id) = observation_id {
  230              let trace_id = self.ensure_trace_id().await;
  231
  232              let mut update = json!({
  233                  "id": observation_id,
  234                  "traceId": trace_id,
  235                  "type": "SPAN"
  236              });
  237
  238              // Handle special fields
  239              if let Some(val) = span_metadata.get("input") {
  240                  update["input"] = val.clone();
  241              }
  242
  243              if let Some(val) = span_metadata.get("output") {
  244                  update["output"] = val.clone();
  245              }
  246
  247              if let Some(val) = span_metadata.get("model_config") {
  248                  update["metadata"] = json!({ "model_config": val });
  249              }
  250
  251              // Handle any remaining metadata
  252              let remaining_metadata: serde_json::Map<String, Value> = span_metadata
  253                  .iter()
  254                  .filter(|(k, _)| !["input", "output", "model_config"].contains(&k.as_str()))
  255                  .map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.clone()))

DIMENSION · COLLABORATION

Collaboration & sub-agents

1 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · 子 Agent 是独立 Agent 与子会话，不是主提示词里的角色扮演

SOURCE FACT

SubagentHandler 新建 Agent，继承/覆盖 provider 与 model，按配置装载扩展和响应 schema，创建 SubAgent 类型会话，并运行标准 Agent.reply；子 Agent 系统提示明确禁止继续创建子 Agent。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

主 Agent 真正启动了另一个有独立历史和工具集的执行循环，而不是在同一段对话里假装分身；但只允许一层委派。

IMPLICATION

独立会话便于隔离上下文、统计成本和回传结果；禁止递归可控制爆炸式 fan-out。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 121–174
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 175–230
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.
