  121  fn get_agent_messages(params: SubagentRunParams) -> AgentMessagesFuture {
  122      Box::pin(async move {
  123          let SubagentRunParams {
  124              config,
  125              recipe,
  126              task_config,
  127              session_id,
  128              cancellation_token,
  129              on_message,
  130              notification_tx,
  131              ..
  132          } = params;
  133
  134          let system_instructions = recipe.instructions.clone().unwrap_or_default();
  135          let user_task = recipe
  136              .prompt
  137              .clone()
  138              .unwrap_or_else(|| "Begin.".to_string());
  139
  140          let agent = Arc::new(Agent::with_config(config));
  141
  142          agent
  143              .update_provider(
  144                  task_config.provider.clone(),
  145                  task_config.model_config.clone(),
  146                  &session_id,
  147              )
  148              .await
  149              .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Failed to set provider on sub agent: {}", e))?;
  150
  151          for extension in &task_config.extensions {
  152              if let Err(e) = agent.add_extension(extension.clone(), &session_id).await {
  153                  debug!(
  154                      "Failed to add extension '{}' to subagent: {}",
  155                      extension.name(),
  156                      e
  157                  );
  158              }
  159          }
  160
  161          let has_response_schema = recipe.response.is_some();
  162          agent
  163              .apply_recipe_components(recipe.response.clone(), true)
  164              .await;
  165
  166          let subagent_prompt =
  167              build_subagent_prompt(&agent, &task_config, &session_id, system_instructions).await?;
  168          agent.override_system_prompt(subagent_prompt).await;
  169
  170          let user_message = Message::user().with_text(user_task);
  171          let mut conversation = Conversation::new_unvalidated(vec![user_message.clone()]);
  172
  173          if let Some(activities) = recipe.activities {
  174              for activity in activities {
  175                  info!("Recipe activity: {}", activity);
  176              }
  177          }
  178          let session_config = SessionConfig {
  179              id: session_id.clone(),
  180              schedule_id: None,
  181              max_turns: task_config.max_turns.map(|v| v as u32),
  182              retry_config: recipe.retry,
  183          };
  184
  185          let mut stream =
  186              crate::session_context::with_session_id(Some(session_id.to_string()), async {
  187                  agent
  188                      .reply(user_message, session_config, cancellation_token)
  189                      .await
  190              })
  191              .await
  192              .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Failed to get reply from agent: {}", e))?;
  193
  194          while let Some(message_result) = stream.next().await {
  195              match message_result {
  196                  Ok(AgentEvent::Message(msg)) => {
  197                      if let Some(ref callback) = on_message {
  198                          callback(&msg);
  199                      }
  200                      if let Some(ref tx) = notification_tx {
  201                          for content in &msg.content {
  202                              if let Some(notif) = create_tool_notification(content, &session_id) {
  203                                  if tx.send(notif).is_err() {
  204                                      debug!(
  205                                          "Notification receiver dropped for subagent {}",
  206                                          session_id
  207                                      );
  208                                  }
  209                              }
  210                          }
  211                      }
  212                      conversation.push(msg);
  213                  }
  214                  Ok(AgentEvent::Usage(_)) => {}
  215                  Ok(AgentEvent::MessageUsage { .. }) => {}
  216                  Ok(AgentEvent::McpNotification(_)) => {}
  217                  Ok(AgentEvent::HistoryReplaced(updated_conversation)) => {
  218                      conversation = updated_conversation;
  219                  }
  220                  Err(e) => {
  221                      tracing::error!("Error receiving message from subagent: {}", e);
  222                      break;
  223                  }
  224              }
  225          }
  226
  227          let final_output = get_final_output(&agent, has_response_schema).await;
  228
  229          Ok((conversation, final_output))
  230      })
  231  }
  232
  233  async fn build_subagent_prompt(
  234      agent: &Agent,
  235      task_config: &TaskConfig,
  236      session_id: &str,
  237      system_instructions: String,
  238  ) -> Result<String> {
  239      let tools: Vec<_> = agent
  240          .list_tools(session_id, None)
  241          .await
  242          .into_iter()
  243          .filter(super::reply_parts::is_tool_visible_to_model)
  244          .collect();
  245      render_template(
  246          "subagent_system.md",
  247          &SubagentPromptContext {
  248              max_turns: task_config
  249                  .max_turns
  250                  .expect("TaskConfig always sets max_turns"),
  251              subagent_id: session_id.to_string(),
  252              task_instructions: system_instructions,
  253              tool_count: tools.len(),
  254              available_tools: tools
  255                  .iter()
  256                  .map(|t| t.name.to_string())
  257                  .collect::<Vec<_>>()
  258                  .join(", "),
  259          },
  260      )
  261      .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Failed to render subagent system prompt: {}", e))
  262  }
  263
  264  async fn get_final_output(agent: &Agent, has_response_schema: bool) -> Option<String> {
  265      if has_response_schema {
  266          agent
  267              .final_output_tool
  268              .lock()
  269              .await
  270              .as_ref()
  271              .and_then(|tool| tool.final_output.clone())
  272      } else {
  273          None
  274      }
  275  }

DIMENSION · INSTRUCTIONS

Instructions & extension surface

3 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factinstructions

01 · 系统提示由稳定骨架、扩展说明和路径 hints 组合

SOURCE FACT

PromptBuilder 聚合时间、工作目录、扩展说明、可用工具、模式、系统 extras 与 hints；工具排序稳定以利提示缓存，并清洗扩展提供的 Unicode 标签。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

提示词不是一整块写死文本，而是像模板一样拼装；插件能加说明，但会先清理可能伪装成系统标签的字符。

IMPLICATION

指令装配应有稳定顺序、来源边界和可测试的净化规则，否则缓存命中与安全都会漂移。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 95–129
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 130–165
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   95      pub fn with_hints(mut self, working_dir: &Path) -> Self {
   96          let hints_filenames = get_context_filenames();
   97          let ignore_patterns = build_gitignore(working_dir);
   98
   99          let hints = load_hint_files(working_dir, &hints_filenames, &ignore_patterns);
  100
  101          if !hints.is_empty() {
  102              self.hints = Some(hints);
  103          }
  104          self
  105      }

  108          self.subagents_enabled = subagents_enabled;
  109          self
  110      }

  113          self.goose_mode = Some(mode);
  114          self
  115      }

  117      pub fn build(self) -> String {
  118          let mut extensions_info = self.extensions_info;
  119
  120          // Add frontend instructions to extensions_info to simplify json rendering
  121          if let Some(frontend_instructions) = self.frontend_instructions {
  122              extensions_info.push(ExtensionInfo::new(
  123                  "frontend",
  124                  &frontend_instructions,
  125                  false,
  126              ));
  127          }
  128          // Stable tool ordering is important for multi session prompt caching.
  129          extensions_info.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
  130
  131          let sanitized_extensions_info: Vec<ExtensionInfo> = extensions_info
  132              .into_iter()
  133              .map(|mut ext_info| {
  134                  ext_info.instructions = sanitize_unicode_tags(&ext_info.instructions);
  135                  ext_info
  136              })
  137              .collect();
  138
  139          let goose_mode = self
  140              .goose_mode
  141              .unwrap_or_else(|| Config::global().get_goose_mode().unwrap_or_default());
  142
  143          let extension_tool_limits = self
  144              .extension_tool_count
  145              .filter(|(extensions, tools)| *extensions > MAX_EXTENSIONS || *tools > MAX_TOOLS);
  146
  147          let context = SystemPromptContext {
  148              extensions: sanitized_extensions_info,
  149              current_date_time: self.manager.current_date_timestamp.clone(),
  150              extension_tool_limits,
  151              goose_mode,
  152              is_autonomous: goose_mode == GooseMode::Auto,
  153              enable_subagents: self.subagents_enabled,
  154              max_extensions: MAX_EXTENSIONS,
  155              max_tools: MAX_TOOLS,
  156              code_execution_mode: self.code_execution_mode,
  157              moim_system_prompt_block: moim::system_prompt_block(),
  158          };
  159
  160          let base_prompt = if let Some(override_prompt) = &self.manager.system_prompt_override {
  161              let sanitized_override_prompt = sanitize_unicode_tags(override_prompt);
  162              prompt_template::render_string(&sanitized_override_prompt, &context)
  163          } else {
  164              prompt_template::render_template("system.md", &context)
  165          }
  166          .unwrap_or_else(|_| {
  167              "You are a general-purpose AI agent called goose, created by Block".to_string()
  168          });
  169
  170          let mut system_prompt_extras = self.manager.system_prompt_extras.clone();
  171
  172          // Add hints if provided
  173          if let Some(hints) = self.hints {
  174              system_prompt_extras.insert("hints".to_string(), hints);
  175          }
  176
  177          if goose_mode == GooseMode::Chat {
  178              system_prompt_extras.insert(
  179                  "chat_mode".to_string(),
  180                  "Right now you are in the chat only mode, no access to any tool use and system."
  181                      .to_string(),
  182              );
  183          }
  184
  185          if system_prompt_extras.is_empty() {
  186              base_prompt
  187          } else {
  188              let sanitized_system_prompt_extras: Vec<String> = system_prompt_extras
  189                  .into_values()
  190                  .map(|extra| sanitize_unicode_tags(&extra))
  191                  .collect();
  192
  193              format!(
  194                  "{}\n\n# Additional Instructions:\n\n{}",
  195                  base_prompt,
  196                  sanitized_system_prompt_extras.join("\n\n")
  197              )
  198          }
  199      }
  200  }
  201
  202  impl PromptManager {
  203      pub fn new() -> Self {
  204          PromptManager {
  205              system_prompt_override: None,
  206              system_prompt_extras: IndexMap::new(),
  207              // Use the fixed current date time so that prompt cache can be used.
  208              // Filtering to an hour to balance user time accuracy and multi session prompt cache hits.
  209              current_date_timestamp: Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%d %H:00 %:z").to_string(),
  210              subdirectory_hint_tracker: SubdirectoryHintTracker::new(),
  211          }
  212      }
  213
  214      #[cfg(test)]
  215      pub fn with_timestamp(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> Self {
  216          PromptManager {
  217              system_prompt_override: None,
  218              system_prompt_extras: IndexMap::new(),
  219              current_date_timestamp: dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %:z").to_string(),
  220              subdirectory_hint_tracker: SubdirectoryHintTracker::new(),
  221          }
  222      }
  223
  224      /// Add an additional instruction to the system prompt with a key
  225      /// Using the same key will replace the previous instruction
  226      pub fn add_system_prompt_extra(&mut self, key: String, instruction: String) {
  227          self.system_prompt_extras.insert(key, instruction);
  228      }
  229
  230      pub fn remove_system_prompt_extra(&mut self, key: &str) {
  231          self.system_prompt_extras.shift_remove(key);
  232      }

  235          &mut self,

  116      pub fn build(self) -> String {
  117          let mut extensions_info = self.extensions_info;
  118
  119          // Add frontend instructions to extensions_info to simplify json rendering
  120          if let Some(frontend_instructions) = self.frontend_instructions {
  121              extensions_info.push(ExtensionInfo::new(
  122                  "frontend",
  123                  &frontend_instructions,
  124                  false,
  125              ));
  126          }
  127          // Stable tool ordering is important for multi session prompt caching.
  128          extensions_info.sort_by(|a, b| a.name.cmp(&b.name));
  129
  130          let sanitized_extensions_info: Vec<ExtensionInfo> = extensions_info
  131              .into_iter()
  132              .map(|mut ext_info| {
  133                  ext_info.instructions = sanitize_unicode_tags(&ext_info.instructions);
  134                  ext_info
  135              })
  136              .collect();
  137
  138          let goose_mode = self
  139              .goose_mode
  140              .unwrap_or_else(|| Config::global().get_goose_mode().unwrap_or_default());
  141
  142          let context = SystemPromptContext {
  143              extensions: sanitized_extensions_info,
  144              current_date_time: self.manager.current_date_timestamp.clone(),
  145              goose_mode,
  146              is_autonomous: goose_mode == GooseMode::Auto,
  147              enable_subagents: self.subagents_enabled,
  148              code_execution_mode: self.code_execution_mode,
  149              include_extensions: self.include_extensions,
  150              moim_system_prompt_block: moim::system_prompt_block(),
  151          };
  152
  153          let base_prompt = if let Some(override_prompt) = &self.manager.system_prompt_override {
  154              let sanitized_override_prompt = sanitize_unicode_tags(override_prompt);
  155              prompt_template::render_string(&sanitized_override_prompt, &context)
  156          } else {
  157              prompt_template::render_template("system.md", &context)
  158          }
  159          .unwrap_or_else(|_| {
  160              "You are a general-purpose AI agent called goose, created by Block".to_string()
  161          });
  162
  163          let mut system_prompt_extras = self.manager.system_prompt_extras.clone();
  164          system_prompt_extras.extend(self.prompt_extras);
  165
  166          // Add hints if provided
  167          if let Some(hints) = self.hints {
  168              system_prompt_extras.insert("hints".to_string(), hints);
  169          }
  170
  171          if goose_mode == GooseMode::Chat {
  172              system_prompt_extras.insert(
  173                  "chat_mode".to_string(),
  174                  "Right now you are in the chat only mode, no access to any tool use and system."
  175                      .to_string(),
  176              );
  177          }
  178
  179          if system_prompt_extras.is_empty() {
  180              base_prompt
  181          } else {
  182              let sanitized_system_prompt_extras: Vec<String> = system_prompt_extras
  183                  .into_values()
  184                  .map(|extra| sanitize_unicode_tags(&extra))
  185                  .collect();
  186
  187              format!(
  188                  "{}\n\n# Additional Instructions:\n\n{}",
  189                  base_prompt,
  190                  sanitized_system_prompt_extras.join("\n\n")
  191              )
  192          }
  193      }
  194  }
  195
  196  impl PromptManager {
  197      pub fn new() -> Self {
  198          PromptManager {
  199              system_prompt_override: None,
  200              system_prompt_extras: IndexMap::new(),
  201              // Use the fixed current date time so that prompt cache can be used.
  202              // Filtering to an hour to balance user time accuracy and multi session prompt cache hits.
  203              current_date_timestamp: Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%d %H:00 %:z").to_string(),
  204              subdirectory_hint_tracker: SubdirectoryHintTracker::new(),
  205          }
  206      }
  207
  208      #[cfg(test)]
  209      pub fn with_timestamp(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> Self {
  210          PromptManager {
  211              system_prompt_override: None,
  212              system_prompt_extras: IndexMap::new(),
  213              current_date_timestamp: dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %:z").to_string(),
  214              subdirectory_hint_tracker: SubdirectoryHintTracker::new(),
  215          }
  216      }
  217
  218      /// Add an additional instruction to the system prompt with a key
  219      /// Using the same key will replace the previous instruction
  220      pub fn add_system_prompt_extra(&mut self, key: String, instruction: String) {
  221          self.system_prompt_extras.insert(key, instruction);
  222      }
  223
  224      pub fn remove_system_prompt_extra(&mut self, key: &str) {
  225          self.system_prompt_extras.shift_remove(key);
  226      }
  227
  228      pub fn record_tool_arguments(
  229          &mut self,
  230          arguments: &Option<serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>>,
  231          working_dir: &Path,
  232      ) {
  233          self.subdirectory_hint_tracker
  234              .record_tool_arguments(arguments, working_dir);
  235      }

L1factinstructions

02 · Skills 采用渐进加载和多生态目录兼容

SOURCE FACT

项目级 .agents/.goose/.claude 目录先于全局目录发现；系统提示只列技能名与描述，真正调用时才载入 SKILL.md 或支持文件，并校验支持文件解析后仍位于技能目录内。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

模型一开始只拿到“技能目录”，需要时再打开说明书和附件，既省上下文，也防止通过 ../ 偷读技能目录外文件。

IMPLICATION

技能系统应把发现、选择、加载分开，并对支持文件做 canonical path 校验。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 313–326
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 327–341
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  313  /// Every directory the agent reads skills from, paired with whether each is a
  314  /// global (home-rooted) location. Order matches discovery precedence: project
  315  /// dirs first, then global dirs.
  316  pub fn all_skill_dirs(working_dir: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<(PathBuf, bool)> {
  317      let mut dirs: Vec<(PathBuf, bool)> = Vec::new();
  318
  319      if let Some(wd) = working_dir {
  320          dirs.push((wd.join(".agents").join("skills"), false));
  321          dirs.push((wd.join(".goose").join("skills"), false));
  322          dirs.push((wd.join(".claude").join("skills"), false));
  323      }
  324
  325      let home = dirs::home_dir();
  326      if let Some(h) = home.as_ref() {
  327          dirs.push((h.join(".agents").join("skills"), true));
  328      }
  329      dirs.push((Paths::config_dir().join("skills"), true));
  330      if let Some(h) = home.as_ref() {
  331          dirs.push((h.join(".claude").join("skills"), true));
  332          dirs.push((h.join(".config").join("agents").join("skills"), true));
  333      }
  334
  335      dirs.extend(
  336          installed_plugin_skill_dirs()
  337              .into_iter()
  338              .map(|dir| (dir, true)),
  339      );
  340
  341      dirs
  342  }
  343
  344  fn parse_skill_content(content: &str, path: &Path, global: bool) -> Option<SourceEntry> {
  345      let (metadata, body): (SkillFrontmatter, String) = match parse_frontmatter(content) {
  346          Ok(Some(parsed)) => parsed,
  347          Ok(None) => return None,
  348          Err(e) => {
  349              warn!("Failed to parse skill frontmatter: {}", e);
  350              return None;
  351          }
  352      };
  353
  354      let name = match metadata.name.filter(|n| !n.is_empty()) {
  355          Some(n) => n,
  356          None => {
  357              warn!(
  358                  "Skill at '{}' is missing a required 'name' in frontmatter, skipping",
  359                  path.display()
  360              );
  361              return None;
  362          }
  363      };
  364
  365      if name.contains('/') {
  366          warn!("Skill name '{}' contains '/', skipping", name);
  367          return None;
  368      }
  369
  370      Some(SourceEntry {
  371          source_type: SourceType::Skill,
  372          name,
  373          description: metadata.description,
  374          content: body,
  375          path: path.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
  376          global,
  377          writable: true,
  378          supporting_files: Vec::new(),
  379          properties: metadata.metadata,
  380      })
  381  }
  382
  383  fn should_skip_dir(path: &Path) -> bool {
  384      matches!(
  385          path.file_name().and_then(|name| name.to_str()),
  386          Some(".git") | Some(".hg") | Some(".svn")
  387      )
  388  }
  389
  390  fn walk_files_recursively<F, G>(
  391      dir: &Path,
  392      visited_dirs: &mut HashSet<PathBuf>,
  393      should_descend: &mut G,
  394      visit_file: &mut F,
  395  ) where
  396      F: FnMut(&Path),
  397      G: FnMut(&Path) -> bool,
  398  {
  399      let canonical_dir = match std::fs::canonicalize(dir) {
  400          Ok(path) => path,
  401          Err(_) => return,
  402      };
  403
  404      if !visited_dirs.insert(canonical_dir) {
  405          return;
  406      }
  407
  408      let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
  409          Ok(e) => e,
  410          Err(_) => return,
  411      };
  412

03
L1factinstructions

03 · 插件 Hooks 覆盖工具、文件、Shell、会话和停止事件

SOURCE FACT

HookManager 从启用插件的 hooks/hooks.json 加载命令动作；PreToolUse 和 Stop 可阻塞，其他 hook 失败默认放行；Stop 阻塞另有连续次数上限。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

组织可以在“运行命令前”“改文件后”“会话开始”“Agent 想结束”等时点执行自己的脚本，但普通通知脚本坏掉不会让整个 Agent 瘫痪。

IMPLICATION

Hook 的失败语义必须逐事件定义；治理门可以 fail-closed，旁路观测更适合 fail-open。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 64–78
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 79–95
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  106  #[derive(Debug, Default, Deserialize)]
  107  struct HooksFile {
  108      #[serde(default)]
  109      hooks: HashMap<String, Vec<RawHookRule>>,
  110  }
  111
  112  /// One rule within a `hooks.json` event entry.
  113  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  114  struct RawHookRule {
  115      #[serde(default)]
  116      matcher: Option<String>,
  117      #[serde(default)]
  118      hooks: Vec<RawHookAction>,
  119  }
  120
  121  /// One action entry under a rule's `hooks` array. We only run `command`
  122  /// today, but we deserialize the others so that loading a plugin which uses
  123  /// them does not fail.
  124  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  125  struct RawHookAction {
  126      #[serde(default, rename = "type")]
  127      action_type: Option<String>,
  128      #[serde(default)]
  129      command: Option<String>,
  130      #[serde(default)]
  131      timeout: Option<u64>,
  132  }
  133
  134  /// A loaded, plugin-bound hook rule ready to execute.
  135  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  136  struct LoadedRule {
  137      plugin_name: String,
  138      plugin_root: PathBuf,
  139      matcher: Option<Regex>,
  140      actions: Vec<LoadedAction>,
  141  }
  142
  143  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  144  enum LoadedAction {
  145      Command { command: String, timeout: Duration },
  146  }

  148  /// Context passed to a hook as JSON on stdin.
  149  ///
  150  /// The `matcher_context` is the string the rule's `matcher` regex is tested
  151  /// against — tool name for tool events, file path for file events, command
  152  /// string for shell events. Other fields carry the same value plus the
  153  /// raw JSON payload of the underlying event so scripts can do richer things
  154  /// without needing to parse a hook-specific schema.
  155  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
  156  pub struct HookContext {

  106  /// Top-level `hooks.json` shape.
  107  #[derive(Debug, Default, Deserialize)]
  108  struct HooksFile {
  109      #[serde(default)]
  110      hooks: HashMap<String, Vec<RawHookRule>>,
  111  }
  112
  113  /// One rule within a `hooks.json` event entry.
  114  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  115  struct RawHookRule {
  116      #[serde(default)]
  117      matcher: Option<String>,
  118      #[serde(default)]
  119      hooks: Vec<RawHookAction>,
  120  }
  121
  122  /// One action entry under a rule's `hooks` array. We only run `command`
  123  /// today, but we deserialize the others so that loading a plugin which uses
  124  /// them does not fail.
  125  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  126  struct RawHookAction {
  127      #[serde(default, rename = "type")]
  128      action_type: Option<String>,
  129      #[serde(default)]
  130      command: Option<String>,
  131      #[serde(default)]
  132      timeout: Option<u64>,
  133  }
  134
  135  /// A loaded, plugin-bound hook rule ready to execute.
  136  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  137  struct LoadedRule {
  138      plugin_name: String,
  139      plugin_root: PathBuf,
  140      matcher: Option<Regex>,
  141      actions: Vec<LoadedAction>,
  142  }
  143
  144  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  145  enum LoadedAction {
  146      Command { command: String, timeout: Duration },
  147  }
  148
  149  /// Context passed to a hook as JSON on stdin.
  150  ///
  151  /// The `matcher_context` is the string the rule's `matcher` regex is tested
  152  /// against — tool name for tool events, file path for file events, command
  153  /// string for shell events. Other fields carry the same value plus the
  154  /// raw JSON payload of the underlying event so scripts can do richer things
  155  /// without needing to parse a hook-specific schema.
  156  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
  157  pub struct HookContext {
  158      pub event: String,
  159      pub session_id: String,
  160      pub matcher_context: Option<String>,
  161      #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  162      pub tool_name: Option<String>,
  163      #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  164      pub tool_input: Option<Value>,
  165      #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  166      pub tool_output: Option<Value>,
